# Taiwan country-facts ledger

Checked: **2026-10-24**. Research-only ledger for 40 countries and seven fact families (280 records).

## Official source register and fetch results

Every evidence URL is listed below, together with official discovery/context URLs and failed fetches recorded during the research. HTTP is the final response status returned by `curl -sL -m 30`; `000` means no HTTP response was obtained. A `200` response containing a challenge, an expired-download notice or an error page is not a usable policy source. Each URL is the exact requested URL, including query parameters.

## Reading the ledger

- `country-facts.json` contains exactly `{country: {fact_key: {value, source_url, quote, checked}}}`. `source_url` and `quote` are parallel arrays: item `i` is the exact fetched URL and its supporting excerpt. Repeated URLs preserve the mapping when a source needs several short excerpts. Each quote is at most 200 Unicode characters.
- The Markdown rows reproduce every JSON value, evidence pair and check date. Original Chinese/English wording is preserved; HTML/PDF whitespace and table-cell boundaries are normalized to single spaces. Quotes split across excerpts remain separate excerpts. Empty evidence arrays occur only for the 22 European countries outside the requested migrant-worker scope.
- `checked` records retrieval/review on 2026-10-24, not the source's revision date or an assertion that every agency page is current. Historical dates, current suspensions and uncertain later changes are stated separately.
- The visa family concerns ordinary passports and nationality-based eligibility, with conditional eVisa/TAC routes distinguished. It does not treat a conditional authorization as unconditional visa-free admission.
- `NO ... listed` and `NOT LISTED` are explicitly identified inferences from a fetched complete official eligibility list. They do not establish a universal legal prohibition. Land-list absence is instead `UNVERIFIED`, because absence does not decide reciprocity. A signed treaty or MOU is not treated as an agreement already in force or an opened recruitment channel.
- The People's Republic of China has only the requested cross-strait regime/statute notation in all seven fields. Russia, Kazakhstan, Turkey and Israel are included in the Asian-country migrant-source scope; the other 22 European countries are marked `NOT APPLICABLE` for that family.
- PDF attachments were downloaded temporarily under `research/raw/` and extracted using `pdftotext -layout`. Temporary downloads were removed after extraction and checking so the only retained deliverables are the two files requested.

## Source discrepancies and version limits

- **Cambodia, eVisa:** [S30](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/cp-181-8051-69fbd-2.html>) still includes Cambodia in its designated group-tour sentence. [S49](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/fp-390-8047-91ef1-1.html>) excludes it, and [S22](<https://www.mofa.gov.tw/News_Content.aspx?Create=1&n=95&s=122673>) expressly withdraws TAC and group-tour convenience from 2026-08-01. The Cambodia row quotes both languages and the withdrawal; the stale English sentence is not treated as current permission.
- **Cambodia, previously issued TAC:** [S29](<https://niaspeedy.immigration.gov.tw/nia_southeast/>) says “若於115年8月1日以前申獲核准者” in Chinese and “before or on August 1, 2026” in English. The precise August 1 issuance boundary is a textual discrepancy; the row does not silently choose one. New applications are withdrawn; grandfathered documents remain subject to validity, supporting documents and entry inspection.
- **Working-holiday partner count:** the introduction at [S03](<https://youthtaiwan.mofa.gov.tw/workingholidayen/cp.aspx?n=6426>) says 17 countries, while its partner table contains 18 entries, including Israel marked temporarily suspended since 2025-06-13. The ledger uses the table and the separate BOCA Israel suspension page [S05](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/cp-153-7839-337e2-2.html>), not the introductory count.
- **Older TAC and eVisa pages:** the 2016 TAC announcement [S25](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/cp-220-1824-1b51b-2.html>) and inactive eVisa page [S14](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/fp-228-4488-8208a-1.html>) were discovery material. Current NIA/BOCA pages supply the ledger values.
- **Marriage-interview revision:** the current BOCA attachment is revised 2026-08-24 ([S48](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/dl-5210-6c88eaff0b5449078bfca7b720878eb0.html>)); the 2025 municipal attachment is historical comparison only. The nationality list is not a list of all visitor-visa scrutiny categories.
- **Land reciprocity:** the ministry-hosted PDF failed to fetch. The official Keelung PDF mirror bears “110.5” (2021-05), and the complete New Taipei official reproduction was also checked ([S28](<https://www.kl-land.klcg.gov.tw/wSite/public/Attachment/01901/f1689584548677.pdf>), [S23](<https://www.land.ntpc.gov.tw/News.aspx?PageSize=200&n=13377&page=1&sms=9730>)). The listed classifications are supported by those sources; a newer country-specific determination was not verified. Do not describe this table as revised in 2026.

## visa_free — Visa-free entry, eVisa, landing visas and TAC

Days refer to the nationality-based visa-exempt stay. General passport validity, return/onward travel and immigration inspection requirements still apply. BOCA's current landing-visa eligibility concerns emergency/temporary passports of visa-exempt nationalities, not an ordinary-passport route: [S18](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/cp-149-271-2fdc9-2.html>) — “Holders of emergency or temporary passports with remaining validity of more than six months for nationals of those countries (except for the United States of America) eligible for visa-exempt entry.”

| Country | Value | Exact source and original-language quote | Checked |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Philippines | YES: 14 days for ordinary passports; trial valid through 2027-07-31. Proof of accommodation, local contact and funds required; crew joining duty excluded. eVisa also described for qualifying government-sponsored events with prior Ecode (not a general tourism entitlement). | [S15](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/fp-384-4485-149c7-1.html>) — “試辦14天免簽證適用國家：&#42;泰國(Thailand)、&#42;汶萊(Brunei)、&#42;菲律賓(Philippines)。”<br>[S15](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/fp-384-4485-149c7-1.html>) — “汶萊、菲律賓、泰國籍人士免簽證措施試辦至2027年7月31日。”<br>[S15](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/fp-384-4485-149c7-1.html>) — “持用泰國、汶萊及菲律賓護照者：須備妥旅館訂房紀錄、在臺聯絡人資訊及適當財力證明以供國境線上查驗。”<br>[S15](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/fp-384-4485-149c7-1.html>) — “汶萊、菲律賓及泰國等試辦免簽證國家機、船組員或工作人員不適用以免簽證方式來臺登機、船工作。”<br>[S49](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/fp-390-8047-91ef1-1.html>) — “應邀來我國參加由中央政府機關主辦、協辦或贊助之國際會議、運動賽事、商展或其他活動者（北韓籍人士僅限來臺參加運動賽事之隊職員），此類人士須由主辦單位（邀訪單位）透過中央政府機關先向領事事務局申獲電子簽證憑證（Ecode），申請人始得上網填寫完成電子簽證申請表。 註：自2026年8月12日起，斯里蘭卡籍人士暫不適用。 ” | 2026-10-24 |

## tax_treaty — Comprehensive income-tax agreements

“Effective year” means entry into force as shown in MOF's effective-date column; first application to a tax period may differ. Singapore's renewed agreement is an explicit example. The MOF also lists a separate EU shipping arrangement ([S12](<https://www.mof.gov.tw/singlehtml/191?cntId=63930>)); it is not a comprehensive agreement and is not assigned automatically to every EU nationality in this ledger.

## marriage_interview — Overseas marriage interview

The nationality-specific process is for marriage to a Taiwan national. BOCA's current country/mission PDF and the procedural rule are read together ([S48](<https://www.boca.gov.tw/dl-5210-6c88eaff0b5449078bfca7b720878eb0.html>), [S17](<https://law.mofa.gov.tw/LawContent.aspx?id=GL000003>)). Paragraph 7 contains exemptions; “YES” does not mean every listed person must be interviewed without exception. “NOT LISTED” does not preclude case-specific checks.

| Country | Value | Exact source and original-language quote | Checked |

## land_reciprocity — Land Act article 18 reciprocity

The exact classification words are retained. Land Act article 18 states: “外國人在中華民國取得或設定土地權利，以依條約或其本國法律，中華民國人民得在該國享受同樣權利者為限。” ([S50](<https://law.moj.gov.tw/LawClass/LawSingle.aspx?pcode=D0060001&flno=18>)). A reciprocal classification is not by itself an individual acquisition approval. All classifications carry the 2021-05 source-version qualification stated in the row.

| Country | Value | Exact source and original-language quote | Checked |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Philippines | 附條件平等互惠之國家 — Natural persons or companies: ownership within 40% (including 40%) of all exclusive condominium units, with corresponding site ownership/superficies shares. Mortgages are allowed beyond those units, but acquiring property on enforcement remains restricted to the permitted condominium rights. Inherited land/buildings are not subject to that 1997 condominium restriction. Classification is from the MOI table dated 2021-05, still reproduced by the official municipal source; no newer country determination was verified. | [S23](<https://www.land.ntpc.gov.tw/News.aspx?PageSize=200&n=13377&page=1&sms=9730>) — “附條件平等互惠之國家 菲律賓Philippines”<br>[S28](<https://www.kl-land.klcg.gov.tw/wSite/public/Attachment/01901/f1689584548677.pdf>) — “表二、附條件平等互惠之國家”<br>[S23](<https://www.land.ntpc.gov.tw/News.aspx?PageSize=200&n=13377&page=1&sms=9730>) — “准許菲律賓自然人或其公司在我國取得區分所有建物全部專有部分 40％以下(包含 40％)建物所有權，並依公寓大廈管理條例之規定准其取得基地所有權及地上權之應有部分。”<br>[S23](<https://www.land.ntpc.gov.tw/News.aspx?PageSize=200&n=13377&page=1&sms=9730>) — “菲國人民或公司得在我國取得不動產抵押權，其抵押物不以內政部 86 年 10 月 22 日台內地字第 8683016 號函所定之區分所有建物為限。但其因行使抵押權擬取得不動產權利者，仍應符合上開函之規定，僅得取得區分所有建物全部專有部分 40％以下(包括 40％)之建物所有權及其基地所有權、地上權之應有部分：如該不動產非屬區分所有建物，則不得取得。”<br>[S23](<https://www.land.ntpc.gov.tw/News.aspx?PageSize=200&n=13377&page=1&sms=9730>) — “菲律賓人民在我國繼承取得土地及建物，不受上開本部 86 年 10 月22 日台內地字第 8683016 號函釋之限制。” | 2026-10-24 |

The brief requests only this regime/statute identification. | [S37](<https://law.moj.gov.tw/LawClass/LawAll.aspx?PCode=Q0010001>) — “臺灣地區與大陸地區人民關係條例”<br>[S37](<https://law.moj.gov.tw/LawClass/LawAll.aspx?PCode=Q0010001>) — “本條例關於大陸地區人民之規定，於大陸地區人民旅居國外者，適用之。” | 2026-10-24 |

Classification is from the MOI table dated 2021-05, still reproduced by the official municipal source; no newer country determination was verified. | [S23](<https://www.land.ntpc.gov.tw/News.aspx?PageSize=200&n=13377&page=1&sms=9730>) — “附條件平等互惠之國家 新加坡Singapore”<br>[S28](<https://www.kl-land.klcg.gov.tw/wSite/public/Attachment/01901/f1689584548677.pdf>) — “表二、附條件平等互惠之國家”<br>[S23](<https://www.land.ntpc.gov.tw/News.aspx?PageSize=200&n=13377&page=1&sms=9730>) — “准許新加坡人民及公司(包括新加坡銀行)在我國取得不動產抵押權。”<br>[S23](<https://www.land.ntpc.gov.tw/News.aspx?PageSize=200&n=13377&page=1&sms=9730>) — “新加坡人得在我國申請租賃工業區之土地或廠房。”<br>[S23](<https://www.land.ntpc.gov.tw/News.aspx?PageSize=200&n=13377&page=1&sms=9730>) — “准許新加坡人取得我國區分所有建物之任何一層作為住宅或商業使用，並得依公寓大廈管理條例第 4 條第 2 項之規定准其取得基地所有權或地上權之應有部分。”<br>[S23](<https://www.land.ntpc.gov.tw/News.aspx?PageSize=200&n=13377&page=1&sms=9730>) — “另如為土地法第 17 條第 1 項各款所列以外之土地，新加坡人得因繼承或遺贈取得土地，並自辦理繼承或遺贈登記完畢之日起 5 年內移轉與本國人，” | 2026-10-24 |

## migrant_worker_source — Migrant-worker source countries — Asian-country scope

MOL's report identifies the six opened source countries in the ordinary blue-collar scheme ([S40](<https://fw.wda.gov.tw/wda-employer/home/download-file/2c95efb38ecb08cb018ecb45a9a800fb.pdf>)). That list alone does not establish nationality eligibility under every special approval in Employment Service Act article 46(1)(11) ([S51](<https://law.moj.gov.tw/LawClass/LawSingle.aspx?pcode=N0090001&flno=46>)); that part is therefore explicitly UNVERIFIED. India's evidence is WDA's government response, not the citizen petition on the same page ([S43](<https://join.gov.tw/idea/detail/65428426-305e-4edf-9f17-53926fc33c97>)). Source-country classification does not verify job-specific quotas or live recruitment channels.

## driving_licence — International driving permits and licence exchange

Both requested Highway Bureau starting points returned HTTP 200 but only an Incapsula “Request unsuccessful.” body ([S24](<https://www.thb.gov.tw/News_Download.aspx?n=561>), [S04](<https://www.thb.gov.tw/cp.aspx?n=302>)). The brief permits marking this family UNVERIFIED when the official list cannot be found quickly. Neither IDP recognition nor domestic-licence exchange is inferred from another country's treatment of Taiwanese drivers.

The Highway Bureau's official index and explanatory page returned HTTP 200 challenge/blocked bodies, not the reciprocity list. ## Facts remaining UNVERIFIED and why

65 country/family records contain a wholly or partially unverified component. India has both the ordinary-source uncertainty and the article 46(1)(11) uncertainty within one migrant-worker record. The lists below identify every such record.

| Fact family / component | Countries | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- |
| visa_free — residual government-event Ecode route | Cambodia | The broad Cambodian withdrawal and the generic event-Ecode clause do not resolve whether a residual Cambodian application is accepted. The English/Chinese group-tour and TAC boundary discrepancies are retained, not silently resolved. |
| land_reciprocity | Nepal, Bangladesh, Pakistan, Afghanistan, Sri Lanka, Iceland, Romania, Serbia | No country entry was located in the fetched MOI 2021-05 table or complete municipal reproduction. No later country determination was verified; absence is not non-reciprocity. |
| migrant_worker_source — ordinary source-country opening | India | The latest directly verified WDA response, dated 2026-06-10, says India had not been announced. The signed MOU is not an opening notice; no subsequent opening notice was verified as of the check date. |
| migrant_worker_source — article 46(1)(11) special-approval / technical-worker route | Philippines, Malaysia, Singapore, Myanmar, Cambodia, Mongolia, Nepal, Bangladesh, Pakistan, Iran, Afghanistan, India, Sri Lanka, Russia, Kazakhstan, Turkey, Israel | The six-country ordinary recruitment list does not establish every nationality-specific special-approval route under paragraph (11). No affirmative or negative finding is made for that component. |
| driving_licence — both IDP recognition and domestic-licence exchange | Philippines, Malaysia, Singapore, Myanmar, Cambodia, Mongolia, Nepal, Bangladesh, Pakistan, Iran, Afghanistan, India, Sri Lanka, Russia, Kazakhstan, Turkey, Ukraine, Netherlands, Sweden, Denmark, Norway, Finland, Iceland, Spain, Andorra, Greece, Israel, Poland, Czech Republic, Slovakia, Hungary, Romania, Bulgaria, Croatia, Serbia, Slovenia, Lithuania, Latvia, Estonia | Both official Highway Bureau pages returned challenge/blocked bodies. No usable official inbound reciprocity list was obtained quickly enough to support a value. |

The seven PRC regime/statute records and 22 out-of-scope European migrant-worker records are intentional scope treatments, not unverified foreigner-rule conclusions. Land classifications that are actually listed retain an explicit source-age limitation even when the classification itself is supported.
